        Idea:
# Plan 1 — Foundation: project scaffold + DB schema + infra

Authoritative spec: `mediator-bot-spec.md` in the project root. Read it in the prep phase. This plan only delivers the foundation that the rest of the system will be built on; do not implement application logic that belongs to later plans.

## Scope (in)
- Python 3.11+ FastAPI project skeleton, async, ready to deploy on Railway. Standard layout (`app/`, `app/main.py`, `app/config.py`, `app/db.py`, `app/models/`, `app/routers/`, `tests/`). Dependency management with `pyproject.toml` (or `requirements.txt` if simpler), `.env.example`, `Procfile`/`railway.json` as appropriate.
- Configuration loader pulling from env vars only (no secrets in code). Settings for: Supabase URL + service-role key, Anthropic API key, OpenAI API key, Groq API key, WhatsApp Cloud API token + phone-number-id + verify-token + app secret, admin password, sentry/log destination (optional), env name.
- All Supabase (Postgres) tables exactly as specified in the spec's "Data Model" section: `users`, `messages`, `memories`, `themes`, `watch_items`, `observations`, `out_of_bounds`, `scheduled_jobs`, `bot_turns`, `tool_calls`, `feedback`. Use `timestamptz` for all timestamps. Include all enum-like CHECK constraints on the columns the spec describes (direction, processing_state, charge, status fields, severity, confidence, etc.).
- All indexes from the spec's "Index Strategy" section, including the partial indexes and GIN indexes on array columns.
- Row Level Security policies as defense-in-depth (service role bypass; deny by default for anon).
- Migration tooling: a single forward-only SQL migration file that creates the schema; document how to apply it against Supabase. Include a teardown script for local dev.
- Daily LLM spend cap infrastructure (counters table or in-memory + persisted snapshot — pick the simplest): `llm_spend_log` keyed by (provider, day) with running cost, plus a small helper `record_llm_cost(provider, dollars)` and `is_under_cap(provider) -> bool`. Wire the starting caps from the spec ($5/day per partner text = $10 total, $2/day vision, $1/day transcription) as configurable. No actual gating of calls yet — just the plumbing and a unit test.
- Health endpoint `GET /health` that returns 200 when DB is reachable.
- Heartbeat: a daily "I'm alive" log line on a startup background task or scheduled job stub (job row, no execution yet — execution lands in Plan 5).
- Monitoring stub: README/ops doc showing how to wire an external uptime check + cron ping to `/health`.
- Phone-number whitelist config (two partner numbers in env), exposed as `is_allowed_phone(num) -> bool`. No webhook yet — that's Plan 2.
- Admin view stub: a single [REDACTED] `/admin` route that returns a placeholder HTML page. Real content lands in Plan 6.
- README with: how to run locally, how to run migrations, how to deploy to Railway, env var checklist.
- Pytest set up; one smoke test per major piece (config loads, DB session opens, health endpoint responds, spend-cap helpers work).

## Scope (out, deliberately)
- WhatsApp webhook handler and signature validation (Plan 2).
- Any LLM calls or tool implementations (Plan 3+).
- OOB guardrail or significance scoring logic (Plan 4).
- Scheduled job worker, weekly summary, decay (Plan 5).
- Admin view actual content, audit endpoints, feedback (Plan 6).

## Success criteria
- `must`: All 11 tables exist with correct columns, types, defaults, CHECK constraints, and indexes per spec.
- `must`: App starts locally, `/health` returns 200, all smoke tests pass.
- `must`: No secrets committed; `.env.example` lists every required key with a brief comment.
- `must`: RLS enabled on every table.
- `should`: Migration is idempotent or clearly one-shot with documented re-run path.
- `should`: README runnable by a new operator without prior context.
- `info`: Spend-cap helpers exist and are unit-tested but unused.

## Notes
- This is a from-scratch project. Working directory has only `mediator-bot-spec.md` and `tool_schemas.py`. Treat `tool_schemas.py` as a pre-existing reference for tool argument shapes — read it during prep but don't modify it in this plan.
- Use Pydantic v2 for settings + models.
- Prefer `asyncpg` + raw SQL or SQLModel/SQLAlchemy with async — pick one and be consistent.

User notes and answers:
- Hard constraint added by user post-init: NO vector store, NO embedding column, NO pgvector extension. Dedup of memories/observations is done via agentic search (the agent reads existing rows before writing) — see Plan 3. Do not add embedding infrastructure speculatively.

        Idea:
# Plan 1 — Foundation: project scaffold + DB schema + infra

Authoritative spec: `mediator-bot-spec.md` in the project root. Read it in the prep phase. This plan only delivers the foundation that the rest of the system will be built on; do not implement application logic that belongs to later plans.

## Scope (in)
- Python 3.11+ FastAPI project skeleton, async, ready to deploy on Railway. Standard layout (`app/`, `app/main.py`, `app/config.py`, `app/db.py`, `app/models/`, `app/routers/`, `tests/`). Dependency management with `pyproject.toml` (or `requirements.txt` if simpler), `.env.example`, `Procfile`/`railway.json` as appropriate.
- Configuration loader pulling from env vars only (no secrets in code). Settings for: Supabase URL + service-role key, Anthropic API key, OpenAI API key, Groq API key, WhatsApp Cloud API token + phone-number-id + verify-token + app secret, admin password, sentry/log destination (optional), env name.
- All Supabase (Postgres) tables exactly as specified in the spec's "Data Model" section: `users`, `messages`, `memories`, `themes`, `watch_items`, `observations`, `out_of_bounds`, `scheduled_jobs`, `bot_turns`, `tool_calls`, `feedback`. Use `timestamptz` for all timestamps. Include all enum-like CHECK constraints on the columns the spec describes (direction, processing_state, charge, status fields, severity, confidence, etc.).
- All indexes from the spec's "Index Strategy" section, including the partial indexes and GIN indexes on array columns.
- Row Level Security policies as defense-in-depth (service role bypass; deny by default for anon).
- Migration tooling: a single forward-only SQL migration file that creates the schema; document how to apply it against Supabase. Include a teardown script for local dev.
- Daily LLM spend cap infrastructure (counters table or in-memory + persisted snapshot — pick the simplest): `llm_spend_log` keyed by (provider, day) with running cost, plus a small helper `record_llm_cost(provider, dollars)` and `is_under_cap(provider) -> bool`. Wire the starting caps from the spec ($5/day per partner text = $10 total, $2/day vision, $1/day transcription) as configurable. No actual gating of calls yet — just the plumbing and a unit test.
- Health endpoint `GET /health` that returns 200 when DB is reachable.
- Heartbeat: a daily "I'm alive" log line on a startup background task or scheduled job stub (job row, no execution yet — execution lands in Plan 5).
- Monitoring stub: README/ops doc showing how to wire an external uptime check + cron ping to `/health`.
- Phone-number whitelist config (two partner numbers in env), exposed as `is_allowed_phone(num) -> bool`. No webhook yet — that's Plan 2.
- Admin view stub: a single [REDACTED] `/admin` route that returns a placeholder HTML page. Real content lands in Plan 6.
- README with: how to run locally, how to run migrations, how to deploy to Railway, env var checklist.
- Pytest set up; one smoke test per major piece (config loads, DB session opens, health endpoint responds, spend-cap helpers work).

## Scope (out, deliberately)
- WhatsApp webhook handler and signature validation (Plan 2).
- Any LLM calls or tool implementations (Plan 3+).
- OOB guardrail or significance scoring logic (Plan 4).
- Scheduled job worker, weekly summary, decay (Plan 5).
- Admin view actual content, audit endpoints, feedback (Plan 6).

## Success criteria
- `must`: All 11 tables exist with correct columns, types, defaults, CHECK constraints, and indexes per spec.
- `must`: App starts locally, `/health` returns 200, all smoke tests pass.
- `must`: No secrets committed; `.env.example` lists every required key with a brief comment.
- `must`: RLS enabled on every table.
- `should`: Migration is idempotent or clearly one-shot with documented re-run path.
- `should`: README runnable by a new operator without prior context.
- `info`: Spend-cap helpers exist and are unit-tested but unused.

## Notes
- This is a from-scratch project. Working directory has only `mediator-bot-spec.md` and `tool_schemas.py`. Treat `tool_schemas.py` as a pre-existing reference for tool argument shapes — read it during prep but don't modify it in this plan.
- Use Pydantic v2 for settings + models.
- Prefer `asyncpg` + raw SQL or SQLModel/SQLAlchemy with async — pick one and be consistent.

User notes and answers:
- Hard constraint added by user post-init: NO vector store, NO embedding column, NO pgvector extension. Dedup of memories/observations is done via agentic search (the agent reads existing rows before writing) — see Plan 3. Do not add embedding infrastructure speculatively.

  "meta_commentary": "From-scratch project: only `mediator-bot-spec.md` and `tool_schemas.py` exist at the working directory and BOTH must remain untouched at the project root \u2014 confirmed via T14. Hard user constraint: NO pgvector, NO embedding columns, NO vector extension \u2014 dedup will be agentic-search in Plan 3. Migration must be re-run safe (`IF NOT EXISTS` + DO-block policy guards); teardown is local-dev only. RLS is deny-anon defense-in-depth; service role bypasses automatically \u2014 do not author per-user authenticated policies in this plan. `llm_spend_log` is operational and outside the spec's 11 tables; comment it as such in SQL. Heartbeat is in-process asyncio only \u2014 do NOT insert a `scheduled_jobs` row because 'heartbeat' is not in the `job_type` CHECK enum; real scheduling lands in Plan 5. Do NOT implement webhook handling, signature validation, LLM calls, OOB checker, weekly summary, decay, admin content, or feedback endpoints \u2014 those belong to Plans 2\u20136. Spend-cap helpers must EXIST and be tested but MUST NOT gate any call sites yet (no call sites exist in this plan). For SecretStr fields use `.get_secret_value()`; admin Basic Auth must use `secrets.compare_digest`. DB-backed tests should `pytest.skip` cleanly when `TEST_DATABASE_URL` is unset so the foundation can be validated without infra.         Idea:
# Plan 1 — Foundation: project scaffold + DB schema + infra

Authoritative spec: `mediator-bot-spec.md` in the project root. Read it in the prep phase. This plan only delivers the foundation that the rest of the system will be built on; do not implement application logic that belongs to later plans.

## Scope (in)
- Python 3.11+ FastAPI project skeleton, async, ready to deploy on Railway. Standard layout (`app/`, `app/main.py`, `app/config.py`, `app/db.py`, `app/models/`, `app/routers/`, `tests/`). Dependency management with `pyproject.toml` (or `requirements.txt` if simpler), `.env.example`, `Procfile`/`railway.json` as appropriate.
- Configuration loader pulling from env vars only (no secrets in code). Settings for: Supabase URL + service-role key, Anthropic API key, OpenAI API key, Groq API key, WhatsApp Cloud API token + phone-number-id + verify-token + app secret, admin password, sentry/log destination (optional), env name.
- All Supabase (Postgres) tables exactly as specified in the spec's "Data Model" section: `users`, `messages`, `memories`, `themes`, `watch_items`, `observations`, `out_of_bounds`, `scheduled_jobs`, `bot_turns`, `tool_calls`, `feedback`. Use `timestamptz` for all timestamps. Include all enum-like CHECK constraints on the columns the spec describes (direction, processing_state, charge, status fields, severity, confidence, etc.).
- All indexes from the spec's "Index Strategy" section, including the partial indexes and GIN indexes on array columns.
- Row Level Security policies as defense-in-depth (service role bypass; deny by default for anon).
- Migration tooling: a single forward-only SQL migration file that creates the schema; document how to apply it against Supabase. Include a teardown script for local dev.
- Daily LLM spend cap infrastructure (counters table or in-memory + persisted snapshot — pick the simplest): `llm_spend_log` keyed by (provider, day) with running cost, plus a small helper `record_llm_cost(provider, dollars)` and `is_under_cap(provider) -> bool`. Wire the starting caps from the spec ($5/day per partner text = $10 total, $2/day vision, $1/day transcription) as configurable. No actual gating of calls yet — just the plumbing and a unit test.
- Health endpoint `GET /health` that returns 200 when DB is reachable.
- Heartbeat: a daily "I'm alive" log line on a startup background task or scheduled job stub (job row, no execution yet — execution lands in Plan 5).
- Monitoring stub: README/ops doc showing how to wire an external uptime check + cron ping to `/health`.
- Phone-number whitelist config (two partner numbers in env), exposed as `is_allowed_phone(num) -> bool`. No webhook yet — that's Plan 2.
- Admin view stub: a single [REDACTED] `/admin` route that returns a placeholder HTML page. Real content lands in Plan 6.
- README with: how to run locally, how to run migrations, how to deploy to Railway, env var checklist.
- Pytest set up; one smoke test per major piece (config loads, DB session opens, health endpoint responds, spend-cap helpers work).

## Scope (out, deliberately)
- WhatsApp webhook handler and signature validation (Plan 2).
- Any LLM calls or tool implementations (Plan 3+).
- OOB guardrail or significance scoring logic (Plan 4).
- Scheduled job worker, weekly summary, decay (Plan 5).
- Admin view actual content, audit endpoints, feedback (Plan 6).

## Success criteria
- `must`: All 11 tables exist with correct columns, types, defaults, CHECK constraints, and indexes per spec.
- `must`: App starts locally, `/health` returns 200, all smoke tests pass.
- `must`: No secrets committed; `.env.example` lists every required key with a brief comment.
- `must`: RLS enabled on every table.
- `should`: Migration is idempotent or clearly one-shot with documented re-run path.
- `should`: README runnable by a new operator without prior context.
- `info`: Spend-cap helpers exist and are unit-tested but unused.

## Notes
- This is a from-scratch project. Working directory has only `mediator-bot-spec.md` and `tool_schemas.py`. Treat `tool_schemas.py` as a pre-existing reference for tool argument shapes — read it during prep but don't modify it in this plan.
- Use Pydantic v2 for settings + models.
- Prefer `asyncpg` + raw SQL or SQLModel/SQLAlchemy with async — pick one and be consistent.

User notes and answers:
- Hard constraint added by user post-init: NO vector store, NO embedding column, NO pgvector extension. Dedup of memories/observations is done via agentic search (the agent reads existing rows before writing) — see Plan 3. Do not add embedding infrastructure speculatively.

  "watch_items": [
    "Hard constraint: NO pgvector extension, NO vector/embedding columns, NO embedding infra in any file. The migration must enable only `pgcrypto`.",
    "`tool_schemas.py` and `mediator-bot-spec.md` must remain at the project root with byte-identical contents \u2014 no move/copy/rename/edit/delete.",
    "Packaging must expose `tool_schemas` as a top-level py-module via `pyproject.toml` (`py-modules = [\"tool_schemas\"]`) so `from tool_schemas import TOOL_REGISTRY` works after `pip install -e .`.",
    "All 11 spec tables PLUS `llm_spend_log` must have RLS enabled with a deny-anon FOR ALL policy. Service role bypasses RLS automatically \u2014 note in SQL comments.",
    "Every CHECK constraint listed in the spec (direction, processing_state, charge, media_type when not null, status fields, severity, confidence, sentiment, health, target_type, feedback sentiment, source, job_type) must be present. `observations.significance` is nullable to support failed-scoring path but constrained 1..5 when set.",
    "All 6 partial indexes from spec \u00a7Index Strategy and GIN indexes on `observations.related_theme_ids`, `observations.supporting_message_ids`, `memories.related_theme_ids`, `watch_items.related_theme_ids` must be present.",
    "Heartbeat is an in-process asyncio loop only \u2014 do NOT insert into `scheduled_jobs` because 'heartbeat' is not in the spec's `job_type` CHECK enum. Real scheduling lands in Plan 5.",
    "`llm_spend_log` is operational and outside the spec's 11 tables \u2014 comment it as such in SQL. Do NOT gate any LLM call sites in this plan; helpers exist but are unused.",
    "DO NOT implement webhook/signature validation, LLM calls, OOB checker, observation/memory writes, or admin content. Those belong to Plans 2\u20136.",
    "Migration must be safely re-runnable: `IF NOT EXISTS` on tables/indexes and DO-block policy guards. Teardown is LOCAL DEV ONLY.",
    "`admin_password` and `supabase_service_role_key` are SecretStr \u2014 use `.get_secret_value()` and `secrets.compare_digest` for the admin Basic Auth comparison.",
    "Phone whitelist normalization is whitespace strip + optional leading `+` strip with exact-string compare; reject empty/None. No E.164 parser yet.",
    "Verifiability gaps from gate (open, minor): the live `/health` 200 against a real DB and the >24h heartbeat log require human/runtime observation \u2014 covered by U2 and U3 after deploy.",
    "Caps wired from settings (text $10, vision $2, transcription $1) \u2014 defaults match spec but must be overridable via env."
  ],

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas

        Idea:
# Plan 1 — Foundation: project scaffold + DB schema + infra

Authoritative spec: `mediator-bot-spec.md` in the project root. Read it in the prep phase. This plan only delivers the foundation that the rest of the system will be built on; do not implement application logic that belongs to later plans.

## Scope (in)
- Python 3.11+ FastAPI project skeleton, async, ready to deploy on Railway. Standard layout (`app/`, `app/main.py`, `app/config.py`, `app/db.py`, `app/models/`, `app/routers/`, `tests/`). Dependency management with `pyproject.toml` (or `requirements.txt` if simpler), `.env.example`, `Procfile`/`railway.json` as appropriate.
- Configuration loader pulling from env vars only (no secrets in code). Settings for: Supabase URL + service-role key, Anthropic API key, OpenAI API key, Groq API key, WhatsApp Cloud API token + phone-number-id + verify-token + app secret, admin password, sentry/log destination (optional), env name.
- All Supabase (Postgres) tables exactly as specified in the spec's "Data Model" section: `users`, `messages`, `memories`, `themes`, `watch_items`, `observations`, `out_of_bounds`, `scheduled_jobs`, `bot_turns`, `tool_calls`, `feedback`. Use `timestamptz` for all timestamps. Include all enum-like CHECK constraints on the columns the spec describes (direction, processing_state, charge, status fields, severity, confidence, etc.).
- All indexes from the spec's "Index Strategy" section, including the partial indexes and GIN indexes on array columns.
- Row Level Security policies as defense-in-depth (service role bypass; deny by default for anon).
- Migration tooling: a single forward-only SQL migration file that creates the schema; document how to apply it against Supabase. Include a teardown script for local dev.
- Daily LLM spend cap infrastructure (counters table or in-memory + persisted snapshot — pick the simplest): `llm_spend_log` keyed by (provider, day) with running cost, plus a small helper `record_llm_cost(provider, dollars)` and `is_under_cap(provider) -> bool`. Wire the starting caps from the spec ($5/day per partner text = $10 total, $2/day vision, $1/day transcription) as configurable. No actual gating of calls yet — just the plumbing and a unit test.
- Health endpoint `GET /health` that returns 200 when DB is reachable.
- Heartbeat: a daily "I'm alive" log line on a startup background task or scheduled job stub (job row, no execution yet — execution lands in Plan 5).
- Monitoring stub: README/ops doc showing how to wire an external uptime check + cron ping to `/health`.
- Phone-number whitelist config (two partner numbers in env), exposed as `is_allowed_phone(num) -> bool`. No webhook yet — that's Plan 2.
- Admin view stub: a single [REDACTED] `/admin` route that returns a placeholder HTML page. Real content lands in Plan 6.
- README with: how to run locally, how to run migrations, how to deploy to Railway, env var checklist.
- Pytest set up; one smoke test per major piece (config loads, DB session opens, health endpoint responds, spend-cap helpers work).

## Scope (out, deliberately)
- WhatsApp webhook handler and signature validation (Plan 2).
- Any LLM calls or tool implementations (Plan 3+).
- OOB guardrail or significance scoring logic (Plan 4).
- Scheduled job worker, weekly summary, decay (Plan 5).
- Admin view actual content, audit endpoints, feedback (Plan 6).

## Success criteria
- `must`: All 11 tables exist with correct columns, types, defaults, CHECK constraints, and indexes per spec.
- `must`: App starts locally, `/health` returns 200, all smoke tests pass.
- `must`: No secrets committed; `.env.example` lists every required key with a brief comment.
- `must`: RLS enabled on every table.
- `should`: Migration is idempotent or clearly one-shot with documented re-run path.
- `should`: README runnable by a new operator without prior context.
- `info`: Spend-cap helpers exist and are unit-tested but unused.

## Notes
- This is a from-scratch project. Working directory has only `mediator-bot-spec.md` and `tool_schemas.py`. Treat `tool_schemas.py` as a pre-existing reference for tool argument shapes — read it during prep but don't modify it in this plan.
- Use Pydantic v2 for settings + models.
- Prefer `asyncpg` + raw SQL or SQLModel/SQLAlchemy with async — pick one and be consistent.

User notes and answers:
- Hard constraint added by user post-init: NO vector store, NO embedding column, NO pgvector extension. Dedup of memories/observations is done via agentic search (the agent reads existing rows before writing) — see Plan 3. Do not add embedding infrastructure speculatively.

        "sed -n '1,120p' pyproject.toml",

    {
      "id": "T7",
      "description": "Write `migrations/0001_init.sql` wrapped in BEGIN/COMMIT. Enable ONLY `pgcrypto` (NOT `vector`). Create all 11 spec tables verbatim (`users`, `messages`, `memories`, `themes`, `watch_items`, `observations`, `out_of_bounds`, `scheduled_jobs`, `bot_turns`, `tool_calls`, `feedback`) using `timestamptz` everywhere, `gen_random_uuid()` defaults, FKs to `users(id)`, self-FKs (`messages.in_reply_to`, `memories.supersedes_memory_id`), `uuid[]` array columns, and CHECK constraints on every enum-like column from the spec (direction, processing_state, charge, media_type when not null, status fields, severity, confidence, sentiment, health, target_type, feedback sentiment, source, job_type). Include `observations.significance int CHECK (significance BETWEEN 1 AND 5)` nullable. Include `bot_turns.system_prompt_version`, `tool_call_count`, `duration_ms`, `failure_reason`. Use `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` and `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS`. Add the operational table `llm_spend_log(provider text, day date, total_usd numeric(10,4) not null default 0, updated_at timestamptz not null default now(), primary key (provider, day))` with a SQL comment marking it operational. Create every index from spec \u00a7Index Strategy including the 6 partial indexes and GIN indexes on `observations.related_theme_ids`, `observations.supporting_message_ids`, `memories.related_theme_ids`, `watch_items.related_theme_ids`. Enable RLS on every table (12 total) and add a deny-anon `FOR ALL` policy on each, wrapping each policy create in `DO $$ ... EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; END $$;`. SQL comment notes service-role bypass.",
      "depends_on": [
        "T15",
        "T1"
      ],
      "status": "done",
      "executor_notes": "Created `migrations/0001_init.sql`. Static verification confirmed it starts with BEGIN and ends with COMMIT, enables only `pgcrypto`, creates 12 tables total (11 spec tables plus operational `llm_spend_log`), includes `gen_random_uuid()` UUID defaults, `timestamptz` timestamp columns, required FKs/self-FKs, uuid[] array fields, all enum-like CHECK constraints, nullable `observations.significance integer CHECK (significance BETWEEN 1 AND 5)`, `bot_turns.system_prompt_version`, `tool_call_count`, `duration_ms`, and `failure_reason`. Verified 17 total explicit indexes including all spec indexes, 4 GIN array indexes, and all spec partial predicates (7 partial indexes present from the spec text: messages raw, scheduled_jobs pending, observations active, memories active, watch_items open, watch_items due_at not null, out_of_bounds active). Verified 12 RLS enables, 12 `deny_anon_*` policies, and 12 duplicate_object guards. Static scan found no `pgvector`, `embedding`, or vector extension references; the only extension line is `CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto;`. `mediator-bot-spec.md` and `tool_schemas.py` hashes remained unchanged.",
      "files_changed": [
        "migrations/0001_init.sql"
      ],
      "commands_run": [
        "python - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nsql = Path('migrations/0001_init.sql').read_text()\nprint('create_tables', sql.count('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS'))\nprint('create_indexes', sql.count('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS'))\nprint('gin_indexes', sql.count('USING gin'))\nprint('enable_rls', sql.count('ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY'))\nprint('create_policies', sql.count('CREATE POLICY deny_anon_'))\nprint('do_blocks', sql.count('DO $$'))\nprint('duplicate_guards', sql.count('EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL'))\nassert sql.strip().startswith('BEGIN;')\nassert sql.strip().endswith('COMMIT;')\nassert sql.count('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS') == 12\nassert sql.count('USING gin') == 4\nassert sql.count('ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY') == 12\nassert sql.count('CREATE POLICY deny_anon_') == 12\nassert sql.count('EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL') == 12\nprint('migration counts ok')\nPY",
        "rg -n \"vector|embedding|pgvector|CREATE EXTENSION\" migrations/0001_init.sql pyproject.toml .gitignore",
        "rg -n \"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS (users|messages|memories|themes|watch_items|observations|out_of_bounds|scheduled_jobs|bot_turns|tool_calls|feedback|llm_spend_log)|CHECK \\(|CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS|ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY|CREATE POLICY deny_anon\" migrations/0001_init.sql",
        "python - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nsql = Path('migrations/0001_init.sql').read_text().lower()\nprint('partial_indexes', sum(1 for line in sql.splitlines() if line.startswith('create index') and ' where ' in line))\nfor phrase in ['direction in', 'processing_state in', 'charge in', 'media_type is null', 'status in', 'severity in', 'confidence in', 'sentiment in', 'health in', 'target_type in', 'source in', 'job_type in', 'significance between 1 and 5']:\n    assert phrase in sql, phrase\nfor forbidden in ['pgvector', 'embedding', 'create extension if not exists vector']:\n    assert forbidden not in sql, forbidden\nprint('checks and forbidden scan ok')\nPY",
        "shasum -a 256 mediator-bot-spec.md tool_schemas.py",
        "find . -maxdepth 3 -type f | sort"
      ],
      "auto_attributed_files": null,
      "evidence_files": [
        "mediator-bot-spec.md"

  "watch_items": [
    "Hard constraint: NO pgvector extension, NO vector/embedding columns, NO embedding infra in any file. The migration must enable only `pgcrypto`.",
    "`tool_schemas.py` and `mediator-bot-spec.md` must remain at the project root with byte-identical contents \u2014 no move/copy/rename/edit/delete.",
    "Packaging must expose `tool_schemas` as a top-level py-module via `pyproject.toml` (`py-modules = [\"tool_schemas\"]`) so `from tool_schemas import TOOL_REGISTRY` works after `pip install -e .`.",
    "All 11 spec tables PLUS `llm_spend_log` must have RLS enabled with a deny-anon FOR ALL policy. Service role bypasses RLS automatically \u2014 note in SQL comments.",
    "Every CHECK constraint listed in the spec (direction, processing_state, charge, media_type when not null, status fields, severity, confidence, sentiment, health, target_type, feedback sentiment, source, job_type) must be present. `observations.significance` is nullable to support failed-scoring path but constrained 1..5 when set.",
    "All 6 partial indexes from spec \u00a7Index Strategy and GIN indexes on `observations.related_theme_ids`, `observations.supporting_message_ids`, `memories.related_theme_ids`, `watch_items.related_theme_ids` must be present.",
    "Heartbeat is an in-process asyncio loop only \u2014 do NOT insert into `scheduled_jobs` because 'heartbeat' is not in the spec's `job_type` CHECK enum. Real scheduling lands in Plan 5.",
    "`llm_spend_log` is operational and outside the spec's 11 tables \u2014 comment it as such in SQL. Do NOT gate any LLM call sites in this plan; helpers exist but are unused.",
    "DO NOT implement webhook/signature validation, LLM calls, OOB checker, observation/memory writes, or admin content. Those belong to Plans 2\u20136.",
    "Migration must be safely re-runnable: `IF NOT EXISTS` on tables/indexes and DO-block policy guards. Teardown is LOCAL DEV ONLY.",
    "`admin_password` and `supabase_service_role_key` are SecretStr \u2014 use `.get_secret_value()` and `secrets.compare_digest` for the admin Basic Auth comparison.",
    "Phone whitelist normalization is whitespace strip + optional leading `+` strip with exact-string compare; reject empty/None. No E.164 parser yet.",
    "Verifiability gaps from gate (open, minor): the live `/health` 200 against a real DB and the >24h heartbeat log require human/runtime observation \u2014 covered by U2 and U3 after deploy.",
    "Caps wired from settings (text $10, vision $2, transcription $1) \u2014 defaults match spec but must be overridable via env."
  ],

  "meta_commentary": "From-scratch project: only `mediator-bot-spec.md` and `tool_schemas.py` exist at the working directory and BOTH must remain untouched at the project root \u2014 confirmed via T14.         Project directory:

        Idea:
# Plan 1 — Foundation: project scaffold + DB schema + infra

Authoritative spec: `mediator-bot-spec.md` in the project root. Read it in the prep phase. This plan only delivers the foundation that the rest of the system will be built on; do not implement application logic that belongs to later plans.

## Scope (in)
- Python 3.11+ FastAPI project skeleton, async, ready to deploy on Railway. Standard layout (`app/`, `app/main.py`, `app/config.py`, `app/db.py`, `app/models/`, `app/routers/`, `tests/`). Dependency management with `pyproject.toml` (or `requirements.txt` if simpler), `.env.example`, `Procfile`/`railway.json` as appropriate.
- Configuration loader pulling from env vars only (no secrets in code). Settings for: Supabase URL + service-role key, Anthropic API key, OpenAI API key, Groq API key, WhatsApp Cloud API token + phone-number-id + verify-token + app secret, admin password, sentry/log destination (optional), env name.
- All Supabase (Postgres) tables exactly as specified in the spec's "Data Model" section: `users`, `messages`, `memories`, `themes`, `watch_items`, `observations`, `out_of_bounds`, `scheduled_jobs`, `bot_turns`, `tool_calls`, `feedback`. Use `timestamptz` for all timestamps. Include all enum-like CHECK constraints on the columns the spec describes (direction, processing_state, charge, status fields, severity, confidence, etc.).
- All indexes from the spec's "Index Strategy" section, including the partial indexes and GIN indexes on array columns.
- Row Level Security policies as defense-in-depth (service role bypass; deny by default for anon).
- Migration tooling: a single forward-only SQL migration file that creates the schema; document how to apply it against Supabase. Include a teardown script for local dev.
- Daily LLM spend cap infrastructure (counters table or in-memory + persisted snapshot — pick the simplest): `llm_spend_log` keyed by (provider, day) with running cost, plus a small helper `record_llm_cost(provider, dollars)` and `is_under_cap(provider) -> bool`. Wire the starting caps from the spec ($5/day per partner text = $10 total, $2/day vision, $1/day transcription) as configurable. No actual gating of calls yet — just the plumbing and a unit test.
- Health endpoint `GET /health` that returns 200 when DB is reachable.
- Heartbeat: a daily "I'm alive" log line on a startup background task or scheduled job stub (job row, no execution yet — execution lands in Plan 5).
- Monitoring stub: README/ops doc showing how to wire an external uptime check + cron ping to `/health`.
- Phone-number whitelist config (two partner numbers in env), exposed as `is_allowed_phone(num) -> bool`. No webhook yet — that's Plan 2.
- Admin view stub: a single [REDACTED] `/admin` route that returns a placeholder HTML page. Real content lands in Plan 6.
- README with: how to run locally, how to run migrations, how to deploy to Railway, env var checklist.
- Pytest set up; one smoke test per major piece (config loads, DB session opens, health endpoint responds, spend-cap helpers work).

## Scope (out, deliberately)
- WhatsApp webhook handler and signature validation (Plan 2).
- Any LLM calls or tool implementations (Plan 3+).
- OOB guardrail or significance scoring logic (Plan 4).
- Scheduled job worker, weekly summary, decay (Plan 5).
- Admin view actual content, audit endpoints, feedback (Plan 6).

## Success criteria
- `must`: All 11 tables exist with correct columns, types, defaults, CHECK constraints, and indexes per spec.
- `must`: App starts locally, `/health` returns 200, all smoke tests pass.
- `must`: No secrets committed; `.env.example` lists every required key with a brief comment.
- `must`: RLS enabled on every table.
- `should`: Migration is idempotent or clearly one-shot with documented re-run path.
- `should`: README runnable by a new operator without prior context.
- `info`: Spend-cap helpers exist and are unit-tested but unused.

## Notes
- This is a from-scratch project. Working directory has only `mediator-bot-spec.md` and `tool_schemas.py`. Treat `tool_schemas.py` as a pre-existing reference for tool argument shapes — read it during prep but don't modify it in this plan.
- Use Pydantic v2 for settings + models.
- Prefer `asyncpg` + raw SQL or SQLModel/SQLAlchemy with async — pick one and be consistent.

User notes and answers:
- Hard constraint added by user post-init: NO vector store, NO embedding column, NO pgvector extension. Dedup of memories/observations is done via agentic search (the agent reads existing rows before writing) — see Plan 3. Do not add embedding infrastructure speculatively.

Verified 17 total explicit indexes including all spec indexes, 4 GIN array indexes, and all spec partial predicates (7 partial indexes present from the spec text: messages raw, scheduled_jobs pending, observations active, memories active, watch_items open, watch_items due_at not null, out_of_bounds active). Verified 12 RLS enables, 12 `deny_anon_*` policies, and 12 duplicate_object guards.
